UID 219 · visionweave · long_final
本地 Qwen3.5-9B 机器翻译；保留原模型内容，不作事实修正。

第 1 段

用户想知道 38:18 时女孩脚底符文的颜色。

1.  **分析 38:18 的场景：** 视频展示了游戏《肯纳：灵魂之桥》（Kena: Bridge of Spirits）片尾/花絮中的一个场景。场景似乎是一个神社或寺庙内部（根据之前的上下文，很可能是山神社）。
2.  **确定主体：** 女孩（肯纳）站在画面中央。
3.  **定位“女孩脚底的符文”：** 她站在一个圆形的抬高平台上。在她脚下正下方，表面刻有发光的雕刻或符文。
4.  **确定颜色：** 平台本身散发着柔和的暖光。这些雕刻（符文）被这束光照亮，呈现出明亮的金黄色光芒。虽然由于亮度过高，光线有时看起来呈白色，但与其他场景（如后来的冰蓝色领域）相比，其整体色调明显温暖且呈现金色。此外，周围的木材和灯笼也强化了这种温暖、金色的色调。

因此，符文是金色的。

The user wants to know the color of the runes on the bottom of the girl's feet at 38:18.

1.  **Analyze the scene at 38:18:** The video shows a scene from the credits/reel of the game "Kena: Bridge of Spirits". The setting appears to be a shrine or temple interior (likely the Mountain Shrine given the previous context).
2.  **Identify the subject:** The girl (Kena) is standing in the center of the frame.
3.  **Locate the "runes on the bottom of the girl's feet":** She is standing on a circular, raised platform. Directly beneath her feet, etched into the surface, are glowing carvings or runes.
4.  **Determine the color:** The platform itself is emitting a soft, warm light. The carvings (runes) are illuminated by this light and appear to glow in a bright, yellowish-gold color. While the light can sometimes appear whitish due to brightness, the overall hue is distinctly warm and golden compared to other scenes (like the icy blue realm later). Additionally, the surrounding wood and lanterns reinforce a warm, golden palette.

Therefore, the runes are Golden.
